**Waves:**

pointAt(route, d):
```js
function routePoint(rt, d){ // rt = {pts:[[c,r]...], cum:[...], total}
  if(d<=0){ const [x,y]=rt.pts[0]; const [nx,ny]=rt.pts[1]; return {x:x-(nx-x)*(-d)/seg0len? } 
```
Simpler: precompute segment list with start points and lengths; for d<0 → extend beyond pts[0] in direction (pts[0]-pts[1]) normalized * -d, clamp at 1.5.
```js
function routePoint(rt,d){
 if(d<=0){const p=rt.p0,q=rt.p1;const L=Math.hypot(p[0]-q[0],p[1]-q[1]);const t=Math.min(-d,1.5)/L;return {x:p[0]+(p[0]-q[0])*t,y:p[1]+(p[1]-q[1])*t};}
 if(d>=rt.total) return {x:rt.end[0],y:rt.end[1]};
 let acc=0; for(let i=0;i<rt.segs.length;i++){const s=rt.segs[i]; if(d<=acc+s.len){const t=(d-acc)/s.len;return{x:s.a[0]+(s.b[0]-s.a[0])*t, y:s.a[1]+(s.b[1]-s.a[1])*t};} acc+=s.len;}
 return {x:rt.end[0],y:rt.end[1]};
}
```
Also direction for eyes: sample point at d+ε.

```js
function solidAt(c,r){ if(r<0||c<0||r>=ROWS||c>=COLS) return true; const ch=MAP[r][c]; return ch==='#'||ch==='H'||ch==='X'||ch==='G'; }

 // try axes in order of desired dir priority: if |desired.x| then x first.

```
Corner checks: 4 corner tiles of bounding box; if any solid → blocked. Radius 0.32.

Snap assist for smooth turns in 1-wide corridors: when moving perpendicular axis is small, lerp pac's coord toward tile center? Classic approach: if |pac.x - (tileC+0.5)| < 0.25 and turning → snap. I'll add a gentle magnet: on each frame, if not currently moving along that axis, ease position toward nearest corridor center by 6*dt... careful it doesn't fight wall slide. Simpler and robust: after collision resolution, if movement is blocked on desired axis AND the perpendicular axis is aligned within 0.3 of a tile center → allow turn (per-axis collision already handles). With radius .32 in 1-wide corridor (width 1), moving straight along corridor works; at corners, per-axis blocking naturally rounds the corner (x movement stops at wall face, y continues) ✓ that's how classic simple implementations do it. Add micro-snap: if |dx|<0.001 and pac.x not at .5 center... skip, fine.

pointAt(route, d):
```js
function routePoint(rt, d){ // rt = {pts:[[c,r]...], cum:[...], total}
  if(d<=0){ const [x,y]=rt.pts[0]; const [nx,ny]=rt.pts[1]; return {x:x-(nx-x)*(-d)/seg0len? } 
```
Simpler: precompute segment list with start points and lengths; for d<0 → extend beyond pts[0] in direction (pts[0]-pts[1]) normalized * -d, clamp at 1.5.
```js
function routePoint(rt,d){
 if(d<=0){const p=rt.p0,q=rt.p1;const L=Math.hypot(p[0]-q[0],p[1]-q[1]);const t=Math.min(-d,1.5)/L;return {x:p[0]+(p[0]-q[0])*t,y:p[1]+(p[1]-q[1])*t};}
 if(d>=rt.total) return {x:rt.end[0],y:rt.end[1]};
 let acc=0; for(let i=0;i<rt.segs.length;i++){const s=rt.segs[i]; if(d<=acc+s.len){const t=(d-acc)/s.len;return{x:s.a[0]+(s.b[0]-s.a[0])*t, y:s.a[1]+(s.b[1]-s.a[1])*t};} acc+=s.len;}
 return {x:rt.end[0],y:rt.end[1]};
}
```
Also direction for eyes: sample point at d+ε.

Path tiles: iterate segments, step 0.25 per cell to collect tiles (rounded).

Pac collision:
```js
function solidAt(c,r){ if(r<0||c<0||r>=ROWS||c>=COLS) return true; const ch=MAP[r][c]; return ch==='#'||ch==='H'||ch==='X'||ch==='G'; }
movePac(dt): 
 let dx=desired.x*speed*dt, dy=...
 // try axes in order of desired dir priority: if |desired.x| then x first.
 attempt axis: newX = pac.x+dx; check corners at (newX±rad, y±rad) tile solid? if clear → apply, else clamp to wall face (snap).
```
Corner checks: 4 corner tiles of bounding box; if any solid → blocked. Radius 0.32.

Snap assist for smooth turns in 1-wide corridors: when moving perpendicular axis is small, lerp pac's coord toward tile center? Classic approach: if |pac.x - (tileC+0.5)| < 0.25 and turning → snap. I'll add a gentle magnet: on each frame, if not currently moving along that axis, ease position toward nearest corridor center by 6*dt... careful it doesn't fight wall slide. Simpler and robust: after collision resolution, if movement is blocked on desired axis AND the perpendicular axis is aligned within 0.3 of a tile center → allow turn (per-axis collision already handles). With radius .32 in 1-wide corridor (width 1), moving straight along corridor works; at corners, per-axis blocking naturally rounds the corner (x movement stops at wall face, y continues) ✓ that's how classic simple implementations do it. Add micro-snap: if |dx|<0.001 and pac.x not at .5 center... skip, fine.

Actually one known annoyance: at T-junctions, per-axis can get you stuck diagonally — acceptable for this scope; corridors are mostly straight lines with L-turns ✓.

**Now let me code carefully.** Let me draft the key tricky parts in my head:

pointAt(route, d):
```js
function routePoint(rt, d){ // rt = {pts:[[c,r]...], cum:[...], total}
  if(d<=0){ const [x,y]=rt.pts[0]; const [nx,ny]=rt.pts[1]; return {x:x-(nx-x)*(-d)/seg0len? } 
```
Simpler: precompute segment list with start points and lengths; for d<0 → extend beyond pts[0] in direction (pts[0]-pts[1]) normalized * -d, clamp at 1.5.
```js
function routePoint(rt,d){
 if(d<=0){const p=rt.p0,q=rt.p1;const L=Math.hypot(p[0]-q[0],p[1]-q[1]);const t=Math.min(-d,1.5)/L;return {x:p[0]+(p[0]-q[0])*t,y:p[1]+(p[1]-q[1])*t};}
 if(d>=rt.total) return {x:rt.end[0],y:rt.end[1]};
 let acc=0; for(let i=0;i<rt.segs.length;i++){const s=rt.segs[i]; if(d<=acc+s.len){const t=(d-acc)/s.len;return{x:s.a[0]+(s.b[0]-s.a[0])*t, y:s.a[1]+(s.b[1]-s.a[1])*t};} acc+=s.len;}
 return {x:rt.end[0],y:rt.end[1]};
}
```
Also direction for eyes: sample point at d+ε.

Path tiles: iterate segments, step 0.25 per cell to collect tiles (rounded).

Pac collision:
```js
function solidAt(c,r){ if(r<0||c<0||r>=ROWS||c>=COLS) return true; const ch=MAP[r][c]; return ch==='#'||ch==='H'||ch==='X'||ch==='G'; }
movePac(dt): 
 let dx=desired.x*speed*dt, dy=...
 // try axes in order of desired dir priority: if |desired.x| then x first.
 attempt axis: newX = pac.x+dx; check corners at (newX±rad, y±rad) tile solid? if clear → apply, else clamp to wall face (snap).
```
Corner checks: 4 corner tiles of bounding box; if any solid → blocked. Radius 0.32.

Snap assist for smooth turns in 1-wide corridors: when moving perpendicular axis is small, lerp pac's coord toward tile center? Classic approach: if |pac.x - (tileC+0.5)| < 0.25 and turning → snap. I'll add a gentle magnet: on each frame, if not currently moving along that axis, ease position toward nearest corridor center by 6*dt... careful it doesn't fight wall slide. Simpler and robust: after collision resolution, if movement is blocked on desired axis AND the perpendicular axis is aligned within 0.3 of a tile center → allow turn (per-axis collision already handles). With radius .32 in 1-wide corridor (width 1), moving straight along corridor works; at corners, per-axis blocking naturally rounds the corner (x movement stops at wall face, y continues) ✓ that's how classic simple implementations do it. Add micro-snap: if |dx|<0.001 and pac.x not at .5 center... skip, fine.

Actually one known annoyance: at T-junctions, per-axis can get you stuck diagonally — acceptable for this scope; corridors are mostly straight lines with L-turns ✓.

Update ghost:
```js
const f = frightT>0;
let sp=g.sp*(f?0.45:1); if(g.inAura) sp*=0.62;
g.d += (f?-1:1)*sp*dt;
if(g.d<-1.6) g.d=-1.6;
if(g.d>=route.total){ coreHit(g); continue; }
pos=routePoint(route,g.d); dirPos=routePoint(route, g.d+0.4*(f?-1:1)); // for eye direction
draw with wobble perpendicular: compute perp of (dirPos-pos) normalized * sin(t*6+ph)*0.06.
```

Collision function:
```js
function moveAxis(pos, axis, delta){ // pos {x,y} grid units
 const r=0.30;
 if(axis==='x'){ let nx=pos.x+delta;
  // corner rows: floor(y-r), floor(y+r)
  const tiles=[[Math.floor(nx+r? wait corners at (nx±r, y±r):
  for(const [cx,cy] of [[nx-r,y-r],[nx+r,y-r],[nx-r,y+r],[nx+r,y+r]]) if(solidAt(Math.floor(cx),Math.floor(cy))) { // clamp
    if(delta>0) nx = Math.floor(nx+r)*1 - r - 0.001; else nx = Math.floor(nx-r)+1 + r + 0.001; pos.x=nx; return false;} 
  pos.x=nx; return true;}
 symmetric for y.
}
```
Hmm careful with clamps: moving right (delta>0), blocked → snap x so right edge at wall face: wallLeft = floor(nx+r) (the solid tile's col); nx = wallLeft - r - 0.001 ✓. Moving left: wallRight = floor(nx-r)+1; nx = wallRight + r + 0.001 ✓.

Order: try desired axis first, then the other — gives natural cornering: e.g., moving right into a T, x blocked → y can still move along the stem ✓ classic feel.

Also add **diagonal prevention**: only move on one axis per frame? with both deltas applied sequentially (x then y or by priority) you get smooth-ish diagonal at junctions — actually applying both axes each frame lets pac cut corners diagonally through wall corners? No — corner checks prevent overlap; diagonal motion in open space is fine and feels good. Keep both, priority = desired axis first ✓.

```js
function renderStatic(){ const g=sctx; g.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0); g.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
 const P=(c,r)=>[ox+c*cell, oy+r*cell]; // top-left px
 // floors
 for(let r=0;r<ROWS;r++)for(let c=0;c<COLS;c++){ const ch=MAP[r][c]; if(ch==='#') continue; const [x,y]=P(c,r); g.fillStyle = (ch==='X')? '#170a24' : pathSet.has(c+','+r)? '#100c2b' : '#0a1030'; g.fillRect(x+1,y+1,cell-2,cell-2); }
 // subtle floor grid dots? skip
 // wall blocks + edges
 const segs=[];
 for r,c if '#': fill block: g.fillStyle='#060b1e'; fillRect(x+1,y+1,cell-2,cell-2);
   const open=(cc,rr)=> cc<0||rr<0||cc>=COLS||rr>=ROWS || MAP[rr][cc]!=='#';
   if(open(c,r-1)) segs.push([x,y,x+cell,y]); // top edge line at y
   if(open(c,r+1)) segs.push([x,y+cell,x+cell,y+cell]);
   if(open(c-1,r)) segs.push([x,y,x,y+cell]);
   if(open(c+1,r)) segs.push([x+cell,y,x+cell,y+cell]);
 // house outline segments (house sits on H tiles; treat H as closed for wall purposes? H is not '#', so adjacent walls would draw edges toward house — good, but house area itself: add its own rect outline): 
 const [hx,hy]=P(5,8); const hw=5*cell,hh=3*cell;
 g.fillStyle='#070d26'; g.fillRect(hx+1,hy+1,hw-2,hh-2);
 // gate gap: draw house fill again over? fine.
 segs.push([hx,hy,hx+hw,hy],[hx,hy+hh,hx+hw,hy+hh],[hx,hy,hx,hy+hh],[hx+hw,hy,hx+hw,hy+hh]);
 // core base ring (dynamic heart on top)
 const [cx0,cy0]=P(7,15); g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx0+cell/2,cy0+cell/2,cell*0.52,0,7); g.fillStyle='rgba(255,60,110,0.10)'; g.fill(); g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,77,109,0.45)'; g.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,cell*0.06); g.stroke();
 // path centerlines
 for(const rt of [ROUTE_L,ROUTE_R]){ g.beginPath(); const p0=rt.p0; g.moveTo(ox+p0[0]*cell, oy+p0[1]*cell); for(s of rt.segs) lineTo(ox+s.b[0]*cell,oy+s.b[1]*cell); g.setLineDash([cell*0.32,cell*0.5]); g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,140,90,0.16)'; g.lineWidth=cell*0.09; g.stroke(); g.setLineDash([]); }
 // neon two-pass
 g.lineCap='round';
 g.beginPath(); for(s of segs){g.moveTo(s[0],s[1]);g.lineTo(s[2],s[3]);}
 g.strokeStyle='rgba(64,84,255,0.22)'; g.lineWidth=cell*0.30; g.stroke();
 g.beginPath(); for(s of segs){...} 
 g.strokeStyle='#5b6bff'; g.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,cell*0.11); g.stroke();
 // gate dashes pink
 const gy=hy+0? gate at top edge: y=hy (row 8 top). Draw two dashed segments:

}
```

```js
function ghostBody(R, wob){ const p=new Path2D(); const yb=R*0.78, n=4, w=2*R/n;
 p.moveTo(-R,0); p.arc(0,0,R,Math.PI,0); // ends at (R,0)
 p.lineTo(R,yb);
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ const x1=R-i*w, xm=x1-w/2, x2=x1-w; const f=R*(0.16+0.10*Math.sin(wob+i*1.9)); p.quadraticCurveTo(xm, yb+f, x2, yb); }
 p.closePath(); return p; }
```

```js
function update(dt){
 // fx always
 for(i=parts.length-1;...) {p.t+=dt; p.x+=p.vx*dt; p.y+=p.vy*dt; p.vx*=Math.pow(0.9,dt*60)? simpler p.vx*= (1-3*dt); if(p.t>=p.dur) splice}
 popups similar (t+=dt, y rises).
 shake=max(0,shake-dt*1.4); redFlash=max(0,redFlash-dt*1.2);

 if(state==='menu'){ for(d of demoG){d.d+=d.sp*dt; if(d.d>d.route.total) d.d=-1.2;} pac.mouth+=dt*8; return; }
 if(state==='over'){ return; } // fx above already ran

 // play (prep or wave)
 frightT=Math.max(0,frightT-dt); if(frightT===0){humStop(); chainIdx=0;}
 
 // aura reset + towers
 for(const g of ghosts) g.inAura=false;
 for(const t of towers){ const def=TOWERS[t.type];
  if(t.type===2){ // bank
    t.timer+=dt; if(t.timer>=3){t.timer-=3; pellets+=4; popup(t.x,t.y-0.6,'+4●','#ffcf9e',1); spark(t.x,t.y,'#ffe135',5); sfx('bank');}
  } else if(t.type===1){ // aura
    for(const g of ghosts){ if(!g.alive) continue; const gp=g.pos; if(dist2(t.x,t.y,gp.x,gp.y)<=def.range*def.range){ g.inAura=true; hurt(g,3.2*dt,false);} }
  } else { // turret0 cannon — both shooters
    t.cd-=dt; t.flash=Math.max(0,t.flash-dt);
    let best=null,bd=-1; for(const g of ghosts){ if(!g.alive) continue; const dd=dist(t.x,t.y,g.pos.x,g.pos.y); if(dd<=def.range+0.25 && g.d>bd){bd=g.d;best=g;} }
    t.hasT=!!best;
    if(best){ const ta=Math.atan2(best.pos.y-t.y,best.pos.x-t.x); t.aim+=shortAngle(ta-t.aim)*Math.min(1,dt*12);
      if(t.cd<=0){ t.cd=def.fire; t.flash=0.08; projectiles.push({x:t.x,y:t.y,tg:best,sp:t.type===0?10:6.5,dmg:def.dmg,splash:def.splash||0,kind:t.type}); sfxShot(t.type);} }
    else { t.aim+=dt*0.7; }
  }
 }
```

**moveAxis:**
```js
const PRAD=0.30;
function moveAxis(axis,delta){ if(!delta) return; const p=pac, r=PRAD;
 if(axis==='x'){ let nx=p.x+delta;
  if(solidAt(Math.floor(nx+(delta>0?r:-r)), Math.floor(p.y-r)) || solidAt(Math.floor(nx+(delta>0?r:-r)), Math.floor(p.y+r))) { if(delta>0) p.x=Math.floor(nx+r)-r-0.002; else p.x=Math.floor(nx-r)+1+r+0.002; }
  else p.x=nx;
 } else symmetric for y with x±r.
}
```
Using leading edge + both cross rows ✓ simpler than 4 corners and works in 1-wide corridors.

**solidAt(c,r):** out of bounds → true; ch=MAP[r][c]; return ch==='#'||ch==='H'||ch==='X'||ch==='G';

```js
function spark(x,y,color,n,spd=3){ for i<n: a=rand*TAU,v=spd*(0.4+rand); parts.push({x,y,vx:cos*v,vy:sin*v,t:0,dur:0.35+rand*0.25,size:cell*0.06,color,kind:'s'}) }

function ringFx(x,y,r,color){parts.push({x,y,vx:0,vy:0,t:0,dur:0.5,size:r,color,kind:'ring'})}

```

Let me also double check **routePoint implementation** with centered waypoints:
```js
function mkRoute(raw){ const pts=raw.map(([c,r])=>[c+0.5,r+0.5]); const segs=[]; let total=0; for(let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){ const a=pts[i],b=pts[i+1]; const len=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]); segs.push({a,b,len}); total+=len; } return {pts,segs,total,p0:pts[0],p1:pts[1],end:pts[pts.length-1]}; }
function routePoint(rt,d){ if(d<=0){ const a=rt.p0,b=rt.p1; const L=Math.hypot(a[0]-b[0],a[1]-b[1])||1; const t=Math.min(-d,1.2)/L; return {x:a[0]+(a[0]-b[0])*t,y:a[1]+(a[1]-b[1])*t}; } if(d>=rt.total) return {x:rt.end[0],y:rt.end[1]}; let acc=0; for(const s of rt.segs){ if(d<=acc+s.len){ const t=(d-acc)/s.len; return {x:s.a[0]+(s.b[0]-s.a[0])*t, y:s.a[1]+(s.b[1]-s.a[1])*t}; } acc+=s.len; } return {x:rt.end[0],y:rt.end[1]}; }
```
✓.

pathSet:
```js
const pathSet=new Set(); for(const raw of [RAW_L,RAW_R]) for(let i=0;i<raw.length-1;i++){ let [c,r]=raw[i]; const [c2,r2]=raw[i+1]; const dc=Math.sign(c2-c), dr=Math.sign(r2-r); const steps=Math.max(Math.abs(c2-c),Math.abs(r2-r)); for(let s=0;s<=steps;s++){ pathSet.add((c+dc*s)+','+(r+dr*s)); } }
```
✓ (axis-aligned only).

Cannon icon path: center (20,24), r15, half-angle 35°: cos35≈.819, sin35≈.574 → P1=(20+12.3, 24−8.6)=(32.3,15.4); P2=(32.3,32.6). Angles: P1 is at −35° (i.e., 325°), P2 at +35°. Going from 325° to 35° counterclockwise (decreasing... in y-down coords "counterclockwise" visually = ... ugh. Let me just reason: large-arc-flag=1 forces the long arc (>180°). Two candidate arcs between P1,P2: short one on right side (through angle 0) and long one through left (180°). I want the long one. large-arc=1 picks it regardless of sweep? No — sweep determines direction; combined with large-arc, exactly one circle... For a given r and endpoints there are two circles (center on either side); flags select which arc: large-arc + sweep uniquely pick. With center fixed at (20,24): the long arc through left is what we want. From P1(−35°) to P2(+35°) via 180°: angles go −35 → −90 → 180 → 90 → 35: that's decreasing? No: −35→−90 is decreasing, then wrap... in standard math with y-down flipped... let me just try sweep=0 first; if it renders the wrong (short) arc visually, large-arc=1 still forces long — actually the combination (large=1,sweep=0) gives the long arc traversed counterclockwise. Counterclockwise in SVG (y-down) is visually... I'll trust large=1 sweep=0 → long CCW arc; if it were mirrored, pac would face left instead of right — minor risk. To be safe let me construct differently with explicit coordinates that avoid ambiguity: use a path with two arcs? Alternative: draw cannon icon as circle + white wedge triangle overlay (bg color) — but card bg varies... simpler: `<circle cx=20 cy=24 r=15 fill=#ffe135/>` + `<path d="M20 24 L38 14 L38 34 Z" fill=(card bg #0b1030)/>` — a wedge triangle from center to right edge approximating the mouth ✓ deterministic! The triangle corners (38,14),(38,34) are outside circle r15 (distance from (20,24): dx=18 → 18>15 ✓ so the wedge extends past circle, clipping visually? no — it's drawn on top with bg color, extending beyond circle edge onto card bg which is same color → seamless ✓. Mouth half-angle: tan≈(10/18)=0.55→29° ✓ looks like pac mouth.

I'll use that technique ✓ safe.

Turret icon: ring + barrel rect pointing up?   </footer>
```html
</div>

<script>
(()=>{'use strict';
/* ============================== SETUP ============================== */
const cv=document.getElementById('game'), ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
const stageEl=document.getElementById('stage');
const el=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const hScore=el('hScore'),hPellets=el('hPellets'),hWave=el('hWave'),hHearts=el('hHearts'),
      hFright=el('hFright'),hFrightT=el('hFrightT'),bannerEl=el('banner'),
      menuEl=el('menu'),overEl=el('over'),pausedEl=el('paused'),
      btnSnd=el('btnSnd'),btnPause=el('btnPause');
const cards=[el('card0'),el('card1'),el('card2'),el('card3')];
const TAU=Math.PI*2;
const dist=(x1,y1,x2,y2)=>Math.hypot(x2-x1,y2-y1);
const shortAngle=a=>Math.atan2(Math.sin(a),Math.cos(a));

/* ---------- maze (left half mirrored to 15 cols) ---------- */
const COLS=15, ROWS=17;
const HALF=['########','#.......','#..##...','#.......','#.#.....','#.......',
            '#.......','#.......','#....HHG','#....HHH','#....HHH','#..##...',
            '#...##..','#.......','#.......','####....','########'];
const MAP=[];
for(let r=0;r<ROWS;r++){let row='';for(let c=0;c<COLS;c++)row+=c<=7?HALF[r][c]:HALF[r][14-c];MAP.push(row);}
MAP[15]=MAP[15].slice(0,7)+'X'+MAP[15].slice(8); // core tile

const RAW_L=[[7,9],[7,6],[2,6],[2,13],[4,13],[4,15],[7,15]];
const RAW_R=[[7,9],[7,6],[12,6],[12,13],[10,13],[10,15],[7,15]];
function mkRoute(raw){const pts=raw.map(p=>[p[0]+0.5,p[1]+0.5]);const segs=[];let total=0;
  for(let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){const a=pts[i],b=pts[i+1];const len=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);segs.push({a,b,len});total+=len;}
  return{pts,segs,total,p0:pts[0],p1:pts[1],end:pts[pts.length-1]};}
const ROUTE_L=mkRoute(RAW_L), ROUTE_R=mkRoute(RAW_R);
function routePoint(rt,d){
  if(d<=0){const a=rt.p0,b=rt.p1;const L=Math.hypot(a[0]-b[0],a[1]-b[1])||1;const t=Math.min(-d,1.2)/L;
    return{x:a[0]+(a[0]-b[0])*t,y:a[1]+(a[1]-b[1])*t};}
  if(d>=rt.total)return{x:rt.end[0],y:rt.end[1]};
  let acc=0;for(const s of rt.segs){if(d<=acc+s.len){const t=(d-acc)/s.len;
    return{x:s.a[0]+(s.b[0]-s.a[0])*t,y:s.a[1]+(s.b[1]-s.a[1])*t};}acc+=s.len;}
  return{x:rt.end[0],y:rt.end[1]};
}
const pathSet=new Set();
for(const raw of[RAW_L,RAW_R])for(let i=0;i<raw.length-1;i++){
  const[c,r]=raw[i],[c2,r2]=raw[i+1];const dc=Math.sign(c2-c),dr=Math.sign(r2-r);
  const steps=Math.max(Math.abs(c2-c),Math.abs(r2-r));
  for(let s=0;s<=steps;s++)pathSet.add((c+dc*s)+','+(r+dr*s));}

const POWER=[[1,1],[13,1],[4,15],[10,15]];
function isPower(c,r){return POWER.some(p=>p[0]===c&&p[1]===r);}
let dots=[],dotAt=new Map(),dotsLeft=0;
function initDots(){dots=[];dotAt=new Map();
  for(let r=0;r<ROWS;r++)for(let c=0;c<COLS;c++){if(MAP[r][c]!=='.')continue;
    const d={c,r,pow:isPower(c,r),alive:true};dots.push(d);dotAt.set(c+','+r,d);}
  dotsLeft=dots.length;}

function solidAt(c,r){if(r<0||c<0||r>=ROWS||c>=COLS)return true;
  const ch=MAP[r][c];return ch==='#'||ch==='H'||ch==='X'||ch==='G';}

/* ---------- defs ---------- */
const GTYPES={
  B:{color:'#ff3b30',hp:42,sp:1.75,branch:'L'},
  P:{color:'#ff9ed2',hp:60,sp:1.55,branch:null},
  I:{color:'#28e6d8',hp:80,sp:1.35,branch:null},
  C:{color:'#ffa94d',hp:100,sp:1.15,branch:'R'},
  M:{color:'#ff2050',hp:520,sp:1.00,scale:1.5,branch:null}};
const TOWERS=[
  {name:'DOT TURRET',cost:50,range:2.8,fire:0.28,dmg:6},
  {name:'POWER AURA',cost:90,range:2.3,tick:3.2},
  {name:'CHOMP CANNON',cost:140,range:3.5,fire:0.95,dmg:55,splash:1.0},
  {name:'DOT BANK',cost:80}];
const CHAIN=[200,400,800,1600];
const PAC_SP=4.3, PRAD=0.30;

/* ---------- state ---------- */
let state='menu',paused=false,T=0;
let score=0,pellets=140,hearts=5,wave=0,prepT=9,spawnT=0,spawnGap=1.2,queue=[];
let ghosts=[],towers=[],projectiles=[],parts=[],popups=[],demoG=[];
let frightT=0,chainIdx=0,pacInvuln=0,pacDying=0,deathAng=0;
let shake=0,redFlash=0,chompFlip=false;
let pac={x:7.5,y:1.5,dir:[-1,0],ang:Math.PI,mouth:0};
let buildType=null,towerSel=null,hoverTile=null,mx=0,my=0;
let best=loadBest();
function loadBest(){try{return +localStorage.getItem('pelletSiegeBest')||0;}catch(e){return 0;}}
function saveBest(v){try{localStorage.setItem('pelletSiegeBest',String(v));}catch(e){}}

/* ---------- audio (created on first input) ---------- */
let AC=null,master=null,muted=false,hum=null,lastShot=0,lastBoom=0;
function initAudio(){if(AC)return;try{
  AC=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
  master=AC.createGain();master.gain.value=muted?0:0.5;master.connect(AC.destination);
}catch(e){}}
function tone(f0,f1,dur,type,vol,delay){if(!AC||muted)return;try{
  const t=AC.currentTime+(delay||0),o=AC.createOscillator(),g=AC.createGain();
  o.type=type||'square';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.max(20,f0),t);
  if(f1&&f1!==f0)o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(20,f1),t+dur);
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001,t+dur);
  o.connect(g);g.connect(master);o.start(t);o.stop(t+dur+0.03);}catch(e){}}
function humStart(){if(!AC||muted||hum)return;try{
  const o=AC.createOscillator(),g=AC.createGain(),lfo=AC.createOscillator(),lg=AC.createGain();
  o.type='sawtooth';o.frequency.value=52;g.gain.value=0.045;
  lfo.frequency.value=3.1;lg.gain.value=0.028;lfo.connect(lg);lg.connect(g.gain);
  o.connect(g);g.connect(master);o.start();lfo.start();hum={o,lfo};}catch(e){}}
function humStop(){if(!hum)return;try{hum.o.stop();hum.lfo.stop();}catch(e){}hum=null;}
const sfx={
  chomp:f=>tone(f?330:250,f?240:180,0.06,'square',0.13),
  power(){tone(180,900,0.35,'sawtooth',0.22);tone(90,520,0.35,'square',0.1,0.05);},
  ghostEat(){[420,620,880,1240].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,f*1.02,0.07,'square',0.18,i*0.05));},
  pop(){tone(300,90,0.16,'square',0.18);},
  frightPop(){tone(500,120,0.2,'square',0.2);tone(700,200,0.18,'triangle',0.13,0.03);},
  shotT(){const n=performance.now();if(n-lastShot<70)return;lastShot=n;tone(820,700,0.04,'triangle',0.06);},
  boom(){const n=performance.now();if(n-lastBoom<90)return;lastBoom=n;tone(150,55,0.22,'sawtooth',0.28);},
  build(){tone(520,520,0.06,'square',0.16);tone(780,780,0.09,'square',0.16,0.07);},
  sell(){tone(700,320,0.18,'square',0.16);},
  err(){tone(140,110,0.12,'square',0.14);},
  select(){tone(620,620,0.045,'triangle',0.09);},
  alarm(){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){tone(920,920,0.09,'square',0.18,i*0.15);tone(620,620,0.09,'square',0.18,i*0.15+0.07);}},
  die(){[300,240,180,120].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,f*0.7,0.12,'square',0.18,i*0.09));},
  waveStart(){[330,440,560].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,f,0.09,'square',0.16,i*0.09));},
  clear(){[523,659,784,1046].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,f,0.1,'triangle',0.18,i*0.08));},
  roar(){tone(70,160,0.5,'sawtooth',0.28);tone(55,120,0.6,'square',0.18,0.1);},
  gameover(){[392,311,262,175].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,f*0.98,0.24,'square',0.2,i*0.2));},
  bank(){tone(980,1240,0.06,'triangle',0.1);}
};

/* ---------- sizing & static layer ---------- */
let W=0,H=0,cell=20,ox=0,oy=0,dpr=1;
const staticCv=document.createElement('canvas'),sctx=staticCv.getContext('2d');
const stars=[];for(let i=0;i<120;i++)stars.push({fx:Math.random(),fy:Math.random(),r:Math.random()<0.85?1:2,ph:Math.random()*7,sp:2+Math.random()*9});
function resize(){
  const r=stageEl.getBoundingClientRect();
  W=Math.max(60,r.width);H=Math.max(60,r.height);
  dpr=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);
  cv.width=Math.round(W*dpr);cv.height=Math.round(H*dpr);
  staticCv.width=cv.width;staticCv.height=cv.height;
  cell=Math.max(8,Math.floor(Math.min(W/COLS,H/ROWS)*0.97));
  ox=Math.round((W-COLS*cell)/2);oy=Math.round((H-ROWS*cell)/2);
  renderStatic();
}
function renderStatic(){
  const g=sctx;g.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);g.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
  const P=(c,r)=>[ox+c*cell,oy+r*cell];
  // floors
  for(let r=0;r<ROWS;r++)for(let c=0;c<COLS;c++){
    if(MAP[r][c]==='#')continue;
    const[x,y]=P(c,r);
    g.fillStyle=MAP[r][c]==='X'?'#170a26':pathSet.has(c+','+r)?'#100c2b':'#0a1030';
    g.fillRect(x+1,y+1,cell-2,cell-2);}
  // wall blocks
  const segs=[];
  for(let r=0;r<ROWS;r++)for(let c=0;c<COLS;c++){
    if(MAP[r][c]!=='#')continue;
    const[x,y]=P(c,r);
    g.fillStyle='#060b1e';g.fillRect(x+1,y+1,cell-2,cell-2);
    const open=(cc,rr)=>cc<0||rr<0||cc>=COLS||rr>=ROWS||MAP[rr][cc]!=='#';
    if(open(c,r-1))segs.push([x,y,x+cell,y]);
    if(open(c,r+1))segs.push([x,y+cell,x+cell,y+cell]);
    if(open(c-1,r))segs.push([x,y,x,y+cell]);
    if(open(c+1,r))segs.push([x+cell,y,x+cell,y+cell]);}
  // ghost house
  const[hx,hy]=P(5,8),hw=5*cell,hh=3*cell+cell*0.35;
  g.fillStyle='#070d26';g.fillRect(hx+1,hy+1,hw-2,hh-2);
  segs.push([hx,hy,hx+hw,hy],[hx,hy+hh,hx+hw,hy+hh],[hx,hy,hx,hy+hh],[hx+hw,hy,hx+hw,hy+hh]);
  // core base ring
  const ccx=ox+7.5*cell,ccy=oy+15.5*cell;
  g.beginPath();g.arc(ccx,ccy,cell*0.52,0,TAU);
  g.fillStyle='rgba(255,60,110,0.10)';g.fill();
  g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,77,109,0.45)';g.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,cell*0.06);g.stroke();
  // ghost lane centerlines
  for(const rt of[ROUTE_L,ROUTE_R]){
    g.beginPath();g.moveTo(ox+rt.p0[0]*cell,oy+rt.p0[1]*cell);
    for(const s of rt.segs)g.lineTo(ox+s.b[0]*cell,oy+s.b[1]*cell);
    g.setLineDash([cell*0.32,cell*0.5]);g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,140,90,0.16)';g.lineWidth=cell*0.09;g.stroke();g.setLineDash([]);}
  // neon two-pass edges
  g.lineCap='round';
  g.beginPath();for(const s of segs){g.moveTo(s[0],s[1]);g.lineTo(s[2],s[3]);}
  g.strokeStyle='rgba(64,84,255,0.22)';g.lineWidth=cell*0.3;g.stroke();
  g.beginPath();for(const s of segs){g.moveTo(s[0],s[1]);g.lineTo(s[2],s[3]);}
  g.strokeStyle='#5b6bff';g.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,cell*0.11);g.stroke();
  // gate (pink dashes, gap in the middle)
  const gy=hy;
  g.beginPath();
  g.moveTo(hx+cell*0.25,gy);g.lineTo(hx+cell*1.6,gy);
  g.moveTo(hx+cell*3.4,gy);g.lineTo(hx+cell*4.75,gy);
  g.setLineDash([cell*0.42,cell*0.28]);
  g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,158,210,0.35)';g.lineWidth=cell*0.3;g.stroke();
  g.strokeStyle='#ff9ed2';g.lineWidth=cell*0.14;g.stroke();g.setLineDash([]);
}
function drawNebulas(){
  let n=ctx.createRadialGradient(W*0.18,H*0.2,0,W*0.18,H*0.2,Math.max(W,H)*0.5);
  n.addColorStop(0,'rgba(70,90,255,0.10)');n.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle=n;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
  n=ctx.createRadialGradient(W*0.84,H*0.82,0,W*0.84,H*0.82,Math.max(W,H)*0.45);
  n.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,64,128,0.07)');n.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle=n;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
}
function drawStars(){
  for(const s of stars){
    const y=(s.fy*H+T*s.sp)%H,x=s.fx*W;
    const a=0.16+0.3*(0.5+0.5*Math.sin(T*1.5+s.ph));
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(210,225,255,'+a.toFixed(3)+')';
    ctx.fillRect(x,y,s.r,s.r);}
}

/* ---------- fx helpers ---------- */
function spark(x,y,color,n,spd){for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=Math.random()*TAU,v=(spd||3)*(0.4+Math.random());
  parts.push({x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*v,vy:Math.sin(a)*v,t:0,dur:0.35+Math.random()*0.25,size:cell*0.06,color,kind:'s'});}}
function puff(x,y,r,color){parts.push({x,y,vx:0,vy:0,t:0,dur:0.4,size:r,color,kind:'puff'});}
function ringFx(x,y,r,color){parts.push({x,y,vx:0,vy:0,t:0,dur:0.5,size:r,color,kind:'ring'});}
function eyePart(x,y,a){parts.push({x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*2,vy:-2.4,t:0,dur:0.7,size:cell*0.09,color:'#fff',kind:'eye'});}
function popup(x,y,txt,color,size){popups.push({x,y,txt,color,t:0,dur:1.1,size:size||1});}
function banner(txt){bannerEl.textContent=txt;bannerEl.classList.remove('show');void bannerEl.offsetWidth;bannerEl.classList.add('show');}

/* ---------- game flow ---------- */
function initRun(){
  score=0;pellets=140;hearts=5;wave=0;queue=[];ghosts=[];towers=[];projectiles=[];parts=[];popups=[];
  buildType=null;towerSel=null;initDots();
  pac={x:7.5,y:1.5,dir:[-1,0],ang:Math.PI,mouth:0};
  pacInvuln=2;pacDying=0;frightT=0;chainIdx=0;shake=0;redFlash=0;paused=false;humStop();
  state='prep';prepT=9;
  menuEl.classList.add('hidden');overEl.classList.add('hidden');pausedEl.classList.add('hidden');
  btnPause.textContent='PAUSE';
  try{if(AC)AC.resume();}catch(e){}
  syncCards();banner('DEFEND THE CORE');hudSync(true);
}
function startWave(){
  wave++;
  const count=Math.min(28,4+wave*2);queue=[];
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++)queue.push(pickType(wave));
  if(wave%5===0)queue.push('M');
  spawnGap=Math.max(0.55,1.25-wave*0.045);spawnT=0.7;
  state='wave';banner('WAVE '+wave+(wave%5===0?' · MEGA!':''));sfx.waveStart();hudSync(true);
}
function pickType(w){const r=Math.random();
  if(w<=2)return r<0.35?'B':r<0.5?'P':r<0.65?'I':'C';
  if(w<=4)return r<0.4?'B':r<0.62?'P':r<0.78?'I':'C';
  if(w<=7)return r<0.45?'B':r<0.7?'P':'I';
  return r<0.5?'B':r<0.78?'P':'I';}
function spawnGhost(ty){
  const def=GTYPES[ty];let route;
  if(def.branch==='L')route=ROUTE_L;else if(def.branch==='R')route=ROUTE_R;
  else route=Math.random()<0.5?ROUTE_L:ROUTE_R;
  const hps=1+(wave-1)*0.30,sps=1+Math.min(0.45,(wave-1)*0.03);
  const g={type:ty,route,d:-1.2,hp:def.hp*hps,maxHp:def.hp*hps,
    sp:def.sp*sps*(0.94+Math.random()*0.12),ph:Math.random()*7,scale:def.scale||1,
    pos:routePoint(route,-1.2),ang:-Math.PI/2,alive:true,inAura:false,flash:0};
  ghosts.push(g);
  if(ty==='M'){banner('MEGA BLINKY INCOMING');sfx.roar();}
}
function rushWave(){if(state!=='prep')return;
  const b=Math.ceil(prepT)*2;if(b>0){pellets+=b;popup(7.5,14.4,'+'+b+'●','#ffcf9e',1);}
  startWave();}
function waveClear(){
  ghosts=[];const bonus=25+6*wave;pellets+=bonus;score+=50*wave;
  banner('WAVE '+wave+' CLEAR  +'+bonus+'●');sfx.clear();
  popup(7.5,14.4,'+'+bonus+'●','#ffcf9e',1.2);
  state='prep';prepT=13;hudSync(true);
}
function mazeClear(){score+=500;pellets+=25;banner('MAZE CLEARED  +500');initDots();sfx.clear();}
function coreHit(g){g.alive=false;hearts-=(g.type==='M'?2:1);
  redFlash=0.7;shake=0.6;banner('CORE BREACHED!');sfx.alarm();
  spark(7.5,15.5,'#ff4d6d',18,4);if(hearts<=0)gameOver();hudSync(true);}
function pacDeath(){hearts--;pacDying=0.7;deathAng=pac.ang;redFlash=0.5;shake=0.4;sfx.die();
  if(hearts<=0)gameOver();hudSync(true);}
function gameOver(){state='over';humStop();sfx.gameover();
  const nb=score>best;if(nb){best=score;saveBest(best);}
  el('oScore').textContent=String(score|0);
  el('oWave').textContent=String(wave);
  el('oBest').textContent=String(best|0);
  el('oNew').classList.toggle('hidden',!nb);
  setTimeout(()=>{if(state==='over')overEl.classList.remove('hidden');},900);}

/* ---------- combat ---------- */
function hurt(g,dmg,flash){
  if(!g.alive)return;
  let m=(frightT>0?2:1)*(g.inAura?1.6:1);m=Math.min(3,m);
  g.hp-=dmg*m;if(flash)g.flash=0.1;
  if(g.hp<=0)killGhost(g);
}
function killGhost(g){
  g.alive=false;const f=frightT>0;
  const sc=(g.type==='M'?500:100)*(f?2:1);score+=sc;
  const pl=g.type==='M'?150:(f?45:20);pellets+=pl;
  popup(g.pos.x,g.pos.y-0.3,'+'+sc,'#ffffff',0.9);
  popup(g.pos.x,g.pos.y+0.25,'+'+pl+'●','#ffcf9e',0.8);
  ghostBurst(g);sfx(f?'frightPop':'pop');hudSync(true);
}
function eatChain(g){
  g.alive=false;const v=CHAIN[Math.min(chainIdx,3)];chainIdx++;
  score+=v;pellets+=45;frightT=Math.min(14,frightT+1.6);
  popup(g.pos.x,g.pos.y-0.4,String(v),'#ffe135',1.25);
  popup(g.pos.x,g.pos.y+0.3,'+45●','#ffcf9e',0.8);
  ghostBurst(g);sfx.ghostEat();hudSync(true);
}
function ghostBurst(g){
  const c=GTYPES[g.type].color;
  puff(g.pos.x,g.pos.y,cell*0.6*g.scale,'#ffffff');
  spark(g.pos.x,g.pos.y,c,10,3.5);
  eyePart(g.pos.x-0.12,g.pos.y-0.1,g.ang);eyePart(g.pos.x+0.12,g.pos.y-0.1,g.ang);
}
function impact(p){
  if(p.splash>0){
    for(const g of ghosts){if(!g.alive)continue;
      const dd=dist(p.x,p.y,g.pos.x,g.pos.y);
      if(dd<=p.splash)hurt(g,p.dmg*(dd<0.45?1:0.55),true);}
    ringFx(p.x,p.y,cell*p.splash*2,'#ff9800');spark(p.x,p.y,'#ff9800',10,3);sfx.boom();
  }else{hurt(p.tg,p.dmg,true);spark(p.x,p.y,'#fff6c9',4,2.5);}
}

/* ---------- pac movement ---------- */
function moveAxis(axis,delta){
  if(!delta)return;const p=pac,r=PRAD;
  if(axis==='x'){
    const nx=p.x+delta,edge=nx+(delta>0?r:-r);
    if(solidAt(Math.floor(edge),Math.floor(p.y-r))||solidAt(Math.floor(edge),Math.floor(p.y+r))){
      p.x=delta>0?Math.floor(nx+r)-r-0.002:Math.floor(nx-r)+1+r+0.002;}
    else p.x=nx;
  }else{
    const ny=p.y+delta,edge=ny+(delta>0?r:-r);
    if(solidAt(Math.floor(p.x-r),Math.floor(edge))||solidAt(Math.floor(p.x+r),Math.floor(edge))){
      p.y=delta>0?Math.floor(ny+r)-r-0.002:Math.floor(ny-r)+1+r+0.002;}
    else p.y=ny;
  }
}

/* ---------- update ---------- */
function update(dt){
  for(let i=parts.length-1;i>=0;i--){const p=parts[i];p.t+=dt;
    p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.y+=p.vy*dt;p.vx*=Math.max(0,1-2.5*dt);p.vy*=Math.max(0,1-2.5*dt);
    if(p.t>=p.dur)parts.splice(i,1);}
  for(let i=popups.length-1;i>=0;i--){const p=popups[i];p.t+=dt;if(p.t>=p.dur)popups.splice(i,1);}
  shake=Math.max(0,shake-dt*1.4);redFlash=Math.max(0,redFlash-dt*1.2);

  if(state==='menu'){
    for(const d of demoG){d.d+=d.sp*dt;if(d.d>d.route.total)d.d=-1.2;
      d.pos=routePoint(d.route,d.d);}
    pac.mouth+=dt*8;pac.x=7.5;pac.y=1.5+Math.sin(T*2)*0.04;return;}
  if(state==='over')return;

  frightT=Math.max(0,frightT-dt);if(frightT===0){humStop();chainIdx=0;}

  for(const g of ghosts)g.inAura=false;
  for(const t of towers){const def=TOWERS[t.type];
    if(t.type===3){t.timer+=dt;
      if(t.timer>=3){t.timer-=3;pellets+=4;popup(t.x,t.y-0.6,'+4●','#ffcf9e',1);
        spark(t.x,t.y,'#ffe135',5,2);sfx.bank();hudSync(true);}}
    else if(t.type===1){
      for(const g of ghosts){if(!g.alive)continue;
        if(dist(t.x,t.y,g.pos.x,g.pos.y)<=def.range){g.inAura=true;hurt(g,def.tick*dt,false);}}
    }else{
      t.cd-=dt;t.flash=Math.max(0,t.flash-dt);
      let bestG=null,bd=-1;
      for(const g of ghosts){if(!g.alive)continue;
        const dd=dist(t.x,t.y,g.pos.x,g.pos.y);
        if(dd<=def.range+0.25&&g.d>bd){bd=g.d;bestG=g;}}
      if(bestG){const ta=Math.atan2(bestG.pos.y-t.y,bestG.pos.x-t.x);
        t.aim+=shortAngle(ta-t.aim)*Math.min(1,dt*12);
        if(t.cd<=0){t.cd=def.fire;t.flash=0.09;
          projectiles.push({x:t.x,y:t.y,tg:bestG,sp:t.type===0?10:6.5,dmg:def.dmg,splash:def.splash||0,kind:t.type,life:0});
          if(t.type===0)sfx.shotT();}
      }else t.aim+=dt*0.7;
    }
  }

  if(state==='wave'){
    if(queue.length){spawnT-=dt;
      if(spawnT<=0){const ty=queue.shift();spawnGhost(ty);spawnT=spawnGap*(0.8+Math.random()*0.4);}}
    else if(!ghosts.some(g=>g.alive))waveClear();
  }

  for(const g of ghosts){if(!g.alive)continue;
    const f=frightT>0;let sp=g.sp*(f?0.45:1)*(g.inAura?0.62:1);
    g.d+=(f?-1:1)*sp*dt;if(g.d<-1.2)g.d=-1.2;
    g.flash=Math.max(0,g.flash-dt);
    if(g.d>=g.route.total){coreHit(g);continue;}
    const p=routePoint(g.route,g.d);g.pos=p;
    const lead=routePoint(g.route,f?Math.max(-1.2,g.d-0.5):g.d+0.5);
    let dx=lead.x-p.x,dy=lead.y-p.y;const L=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;dx/=L;dy/=L;
    g.ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  }

  for(let i=projectiles.length-1;i>=0;i--){const p=projectiles[i];
    if(!p.tg||!p.tg.alive){spark(p.x,p.y,'#8b96c9',2,1.5);projectiles.splice(i,1);continue;}
    const dx=p.tg.pos.x-p.x,dy=p.tg.pos.y-p.y,dd=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(dd<0.3){impact(p);projectiles.splice(i,1);continue;}
    p.x+=dx/dd*p.sp*dt;p.y+=dy/dd*p.sp*dt;p.life+=dt;
    if(p.life>4)projectiles.splice(i,1);}

  pacInvuln=Math.max(0,pacInvuln-dt);
  if(pacDying>0){pacDying-=dt;deathAng+=dt*16;
    if(pacDying<=0){pac.x=7.5;pac.y=1.5;pac.ang=Math.PI;pacInvuln=2.5;}
  }else{
    const sp=PAC_SP*dt,dx=pac.dir[0]*sp,dy=pac.dir[1]*sp;
    if(pac.dir[0]!==0){moveAxis('x',dx);moveAxis('y',dy);}
    else{moveAxis('y',dy);moveAxis('x',dx);}
    const moving=(pac.dir[0]!==0||pac.dir[1]!==0);
    if(moving){pac.mouth+=dt*16;
      const va=Math.atan2(pac.dir[1],pac.dir[0]);
      pac.ang+=shortAngle(va-pac.ang)*Math.min(1,dt*14);}
    // eat dots
    const dot=dotAt.get(Math.floor(pac.x)+','+Math.floor(pac.y));
    if(dot&&dot.alive){dot.alive=false;dotsLeft--;
      if(dot.pow){frightT=7.5;chainIdx=0;humStart();score+=50;
        banner('POWER!');sfx.power();ringFx(pac.x,pac.y,cell*2.4,'#3d6bff');
        spark(pac.x,pac.y,'#ffb457',12,3);}
      else{score+=10;chompFlip=!chompFlip;sfx.chomp(chompFlip);spark(pac.x,pac.y,'#ffcf9e',3,1.6);}
      if(dotsLeft<=0)mazeClear();}
    // ghost contact
    for(const g of ghosts){if(!g.alive)continue;
      const dd=dist(pac.x,pac.y,g.pos.x,g.pos.y),cr=0.58+0.2*g.scale;
      if(dd<cr){if(frightT>0)eatChain(g);
        else if(pacInvuln<=0){pacDeath();break;}}}
  }
}

/* ---------- drawing ---------- */
function ghostBody(R,phase){
  const p=new Path2D(),yb=R*0.78,n=4,w=2*R/n;
  p.moveTo(-R,0);p.arc(0,0,R,Math.PI,0);p.lineTo(R,yb);
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x1=R-i*w,xm=x1-w/2,x2=x1-w;
    const f=R*(0.16+0.1*Math.sin(phase+i*1.9));
    p.quadraticCurveTo(xm,yb+f,x2,yb);}
  p.closePath();return p;
}
function drawGhost(g){
  const f=frightT>0&&state!=='menu';
  const wob=Math.sin(T*5+g.ph)*0.055;
  const px=ox+g.pos.x*cell-Math.sin(g.ang)*wob*cell;
  const py=oy+g.pos.y*cell+Math.cos(g.ang)*wob*cell;
  const R=cell*0.46*g.scale;
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(px,py);
  const body=ghostBody(R,T*7+g.ph);
  let col=f?((frightT<2&&(Math.floor(T*8)%2===0))?'#f4f6ff':'#3d6bff'):GTYPES[g.type].color;
  ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.fill(body);
  if(g.type==='M'){ctx.lineWidth=R*0.16;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.85)';ctx.stroke(body);}
  if(g.flash>0){ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+Math.min(0.75,g.flash*6).toFixed(2)+')';ctx.fill(body);}
  const ex=R*0.42,ey=-R*0.16;
  if(f){
    ctx.fillStyle='#fff';
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(-ex,-R*0.1,R*0.13,0,TAU);ctx.arc(ex,-R*0.1,R*0.13,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#fff';ctx.lineWidth=R*0.13;ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.beginPath();
    let zy=R*0.30;ctx.moveTo(-R*0.52,zy);
    for(let i=1;i<=4;i++)ctx.lineTo(-R*0.52+i*(R*1.04/4),zy+(i%2?-R*0.14:R*0.14));
    ctx.stroke();
  }else{
    ctx.fillStyle='#fff';
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(-ex,ey,R*0.3,R*0.38,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(ex,ey,R*0.3,R*0.38,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    const pox=Math.cos(g.ang)*R*0.14,poy=Math.sin(g.ang)*R*0.14;
    ctx.fillStyle='#12205e';
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(-ex+pox,ey+poy,R*0.16,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ex+pox,ey+poy,R*0.16,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    if(g.type==='B'){ctx.strokeStyle='#7a1010';ctx.lineWidth=R*0.13;ctx.lineCap='round';
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-ex-R*0.34,ey-R*0.52);ctx.lineTo(-ex+R*0.1,ey-R*0.3);
      ctx.moveTo(ex+R*0.34,ey-R*0.52);ctx.lineTo(ex-R*0.1,ey-R*0.3);ctx.stroke();}
  }
  ctx.restore();
  if(g.maxHp&&g.hp<g.maxHp-0.5){const bw=R*2.2,ratio=Math.max(0,g.hp/g.maxHp);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(5,8,20,0.7)';ctx.fillRect(px-bw/2,py-R-cell*0.32,bw,4);
    ctx.fillStyle=ratio>0.5?'#7dff6a':ratio>0.25?'#ffd23f':'#ff5050';
    ctx.fillRect(px-bw/2,py-R-cell*0.32,bw*ratio,4);}
}
function drawDots(){
  for(const d of dots){if(!d.alive)continue;
    const x=ox+(d.c+0.5)*cell,y=oy+(d.r+0.5)*cell;
    if(d.pow){const r=cell*(0.26+0.05*Math.sin(T*6+d.c));
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,180,107,0.2)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r*2,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffb46b';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
    else{ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,207,158,'+(0.72+0.25*Math.sin(T*3+d.r)).toFixed(2)+')';
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,cell*0.11,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}}
}
function drawAuras(){
  for(const t of towers){if(t.type!==1)continue;const def=TOWERS[1];
    const x=ox+t.x*cell,y=oy+t.y*cell,R=def.range*cell;
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,180,87,0.05)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,R,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const p=((T*0.6+i*0.5)%1);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,180,87,'+((1-p)*0.3).toFixed(3)+')';ctx.lineWidth=2;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,p*R,0,TAU);ctx.stroke();}}
}
function drawTowerIcon(type,x,y,t,alpha){
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha==null?1:alpha;ctx.translate(x,y);
  if(type===0){ // dot turret
    ctx.fillStyle='#141a3f';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,cell*0.34,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#5b6bff';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,cell*0.07);ctx.stroke();
    const a=t?t.aim:-Math.PI/2;
    ctx.strokeStyle='#ffd23f';ctx.lineWidth=cell*0.13;ctx.lineCap='round';
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,0);ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*cell*0.42,Math.sin(a)*cell*0.42);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='#ffe135';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,cell*0.13,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    if(t&&t.flash>0){ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(Math.cos(a)*cell*0.46,Math.sin(a)*cell*0.46,cell*0.12,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
  }else if(type===1){ // power aura
    const r=cell*0.3*(1+0.1*Math.sin(T*5));
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,180,87,0.25)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,r*1.9,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle='#ffb457';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
  }else if(type===2){ // chomp cannon (a little pac)
    const a=t?t.aim:-Math.PI/2;
    const half=(t&&t.flash>0)?0.85:0.34+0.1*Math.sin(T*3);
    ctx.rotate(a);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,225,53,0.15)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,cell*0.55,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,0);ctx.arc(0,0,cell*0.42,half,TAU-half);ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle='#ffe135';ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(180,120,0,0.7)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,cell*0.05);ctx.stroke();
  }else{ // dot bank
    const w=cell*0.62,h=cell*0.56;
    ctx.fillStyle='#141a3f';ctx.strokeStyle='#5b6bff';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,cell*0.07);
    ctx.beginPath();
    if(ctx.roundRect)ctx.roundRect(-w/2,-h/2,w,h,cell*0.1);else ctx.rect(-w/2,-h/2,w,h);
    ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    const pr=t?Math.min(1,t.timer/3):1;
    ctx.fillStyle='#ffe135';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,cell*0.08+cell*0.14*pr,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
function drawTowers(){
  for(const t of towers){
    const x=ox+t.x*cell,y=oy+t.y*cell;
    if(t===towerSel||hoverTile&&t.c===hoverTile[0]&&t.r===hoverTile[1]){
      const def=TOWERS[t.type];
      if(def.range){ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,225,53,0.7)';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;
        ctx.setLineDash([6,5]);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,def.range*cell,0,TAU);ctx.stroke();ctx.setLineDash([]);
        ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,225,53,0.06)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,def.range*cell,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}}
    drawTowerIcon(t.type,x,y,t,1);}
}
function drawProjectiles(){
  for(const p of projectiles){const x=ox+p.x*cell,y=oy+p.y*cell;
    if(p.kind===0){
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,246,201,0.5)';ctx.lineWidth=cell*0.08;ctx.lineCap='round';
      const dx=p.tg?Math.sign(p.tg.pos.x-p.x):1,dy=p.tg?Math.sign(p.tg.pos.y-p.y):0;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y);ctx.lineTo(x-dx*cell*0.3,y-dy*cell*0.3);ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle='#fff6c9';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,cell*0.1,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    }else{
      ctx.fillStyle='#ff9800';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,cell*0.2,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffe9b0';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,cell*0.11,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      const a=T*6;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(120,50,0,0.8)';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x+Math.cos(a)*cell*0.17,y+Math.sin(a)*cell*0.17);
      ctx.lineTo(x-Math.cos(a)*cell*0.17,y-Math.sin(a)*cell*0.17);ctx.stroke();}
  }
}
function drawCore(){
  const cx=ox+7.5*cell,cy=oy+15.5*cell;
  const s=cell*(0.34+0.05*Math.sin(T*3.2));
  const heart=(k)=>{ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(cx,cy+s*0.85*k);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx-s*1.4*k,cy-s*0.15*k,cx-s*0.7*k,cy-s*1.05*k,cx,cy-s*0.35*k);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx+s*0.7*k,cy-s*1.05*k,cx+s*1.4*k,cy-s*0.15*k,cx,cy+s*0.85*k);ctx.closePath();};
  heart(1.35);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,77,109,0.2)';ctx.fill();
  heart(1);ctx.fillStyle='#ff4d6d';ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.55)';
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx-s*0.35,cy-s*0.35,s*0.18,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
}
function drawHouse(){
  const[hx,hy]=P58();const hw=5*cell,hh=3*cell+cell*0.35;
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(7,13,38,0.92)';ctx.fillRect(hx+1,hy+1,hw-2,hh-2);
  ctx.strokeStyle='#5b6bff';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,cell*0.11);ctx.lineCap='round';
  ctx.strokeRect(hx,hy,hw,hh);
}
function P58(){return[ox+5*cell,oy+8*cell];}
function drawPac(){
  if(state==='over'&&pacDying<=0&&hearts<=0)return;
  const x=ox+pac.x*cell,y=oy+pac.y*cell;
  let R=cell*0.44,ang=pac.ang,alpha=1;
  if(pacDying>0){const k=Math.max(0.05,pacDying/0.7);R*=k;ang=deathAng;alpha=k;}
  else if(pacInvuln>0&&(Math.floor(T*12)%2===0))alpha=0.35;
  const moving=(state==='prep'||state==='wave')&&pacDying<=0&&(pac.dir[0]||pac.dir[1]);
  const half=pacDying>0?(1-alpha)*1.4:(moving?0.12+0.4*Math.abs(Math.sin(pac.mouth)):0.18+0.1*Math.sin(T*3));
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(ang);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,225,53,0.14)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,R*1.3,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,0);ctx.arc(0,0,R,half,TAU-half);ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle='#ffe135';ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,244,180,0.8)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,R*0.07);ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}
function drawParts(){
  for(const p of parts){const x=ox+p.x*cell,y=oy+p.y*cell,k=p.t/p.dur;
    if(p.kind==='s'){ctx.globalAlpha=1-k;ctx.fillStyle=p.color;
      ctx.fillRect(x-p.size/2,y-p.size/2,p.size,p.size);}
    else if(p.kind==='puff'){ctx.globalAlpha=(1-k)*0.7;ctx.strokeStyle=p.color;ctx.lineWidth=3;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,p.size*(0.4+k),0,TAU);ctx.stroke();}
    else if(p.kind==='ring'){ctx.globalAlpha=(1-k)*0.8;ctx.strokeStyle=p.color;ctx.lineWidth=2.5;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,p.size*k+2,0,TAU);ctx.stroke();}
    else if(p.kind==='eye'){ctx.globalAlpha=1-k;
      ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,p.size,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#12205e';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,p.size*0.45,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;}
}
function drawPopups(){
  ctx.textAlign='center';
  for(const p of popups){const k=p.t/p.dur;
    const x=ox+p.x*cell,y=oy+(p.y-k*0.9)*cell;
    ctx.globalAlpha=1-k*k;
    ctx.font='700 '+Math.max(10,cell*0.34*p.size)+'px Consolas,Menlo,monospace';
    ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(5,8,20,0.85)';ctx.strokeText(p.txt,x,y);
    ctx.fillStyle=p.color;ctx.fillText(p.txt,x,y);}
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;
}
function drawBuildPreview(){
  if(buildType==null||!hoverTile)return;
  const[c,r]=hoverTile;if(c<0||r<0||c>=COLS||r>=ROWS)return;
  const def=TOWERS[buildType];
  const ok=MAP[r][c]==='.'&&!pathSet.has(c+','+r)&&!towerAt(c,r)&&pellets>=def.cost;
  const x=ox+c*cell,y=oy+r*cell,ccx=x+cell/2,ccy=y+cell/2;
  ctx.fillStyle=ok?'rgba(58,255,140,0.22)':'rgba(255,80,90,0.22)';
  ctx.fillRect(x+1,y+1,cell-2,cell-2);
  if(def.range){ctx.strokeStyle=ok?'rgba(255,225,53,0.6)':'rgba(255,80,90,0.5)';
    ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.setLineDash([5,5]);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ccx,ccy,def.range*cell,0,TAU);ctx.stroke();ctx.setLineDash([]);}
  drawTowerIcon(buildType,ccx,ccy,null,0.55);
}
function drawSelInfo(){
  if(!towerSel)return;const def=TOWERS[towerSel.type];
  const x=ox+towerSel.x*cell,y=oy+towerSel.y*cell;
  const v=Math.floor(def.cost*0.7);
  ctx.font='700 '+Math.max(10,cell*0.3)+'px Consolas,Menlo,monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
  const txt='SELL [X] +'+v+'●';
  let tx=Math.min(Math.max(x,cell*1.4),W-cell*1.4),ty=y-cell*0.9;
  ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(5,8,20,0.9)';ctx.strokeText(txt,tx,ty);
  ctx.fillStyle='#ffe135';ctx.fillText(txt,tx,ty);
}
function draw(){
  ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
  drawNebulas();drawStars();
  ctx.save();
  if(shake>0.01)ctx.translate((Math.random()-0.5)*shake*16,(Math.random()-0.5)*shake*16);
  ctx.drawImage(staticCv,0,0,W,H);
  drawDots();drawAuras();drawTowers();drawProjectiles();
  if(state==='menu')for(const g of demoG)drawGhost(g);
  else for(const g of ghosts)if(g.alive)drawGhost(g);
  drawCore();drawHouse();drawPac();
  drawParts();drawPopups();drawBuildPreview();drawSelInfo();
  ctx.restore();
  if(redFlash>0.01){ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,40,70,'+(redFlash*0.3).toFixed(3)+')';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);}
  if(frightT>0&&(state==='prep'||state==='wave')){
    const gr=ctx.createRadialGradient(W/2,H/2,Math.min(W,H)*0.35,W/2,H/2,Math.max(W,H)*0.72);
    gr.addColorStop(0,'rgba(47,107,255,0)');gr.addColorStop(1,'rgba(47,107,255,0.16)');
    ctx.fillStyle=gr;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);}
  const megaAlive=ghosts.some(g=>g.alive&&g.type==='M');
  if(megaAlive&&(state==='wave'||state==='prep')){
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,32,80,'+(0.2+0.18*Math.sin(T*6)).toFixed(3)+')';ctx.lineWidth=3;
    ctx.strokeRect(ox-4,oy-4,COLS*cell+8,ROWS*cell+8);}
  ctx.textAlign='center';
  if(state==='prep'){
    const s=Math.ceil(prepT),rush=Math.ceil(prepT)*2;
    const y=oy>36?oy-12:18;
    ctx.font='600 '+Math.max(11,cell*0.32)+'px Consolas,Menlo,monospace';
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(5,8,20,0.7)';
    const t1='WAVE '+(wave+1)+' IN '+s+'s   [SPACE] RUSH NOW +'+rush+'●';
    ctx.fillText(t1,W/2+1,y+1);ctx.fillStyle='#aab4e4';ctx.fillText(t1,W/2,y);
    if(wave===0){ctx.font='600 '+Math.max(9,cell*0.24)+'px Consolas,Menlo,monospace';
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(139,150,201,0.85)';
      ctx.fillText('BUILD TOWERS ON CORRIDORS — NOT THE GHOST LANES',W/2,y+cell*0.42);}
  }
  if(state==='wave'){
    const alive=queue.length+ghosts.filter(g=>g.alive).length;
    ctx.textAlign='right';ctx.font='600 '+Math.max(10,cell*0.28)+'px Consolas,Menlo,monospace';
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(139,150,201,0.7)';ctx.fillText('GHOSTS '+alive,W-10,20);ctx.textAlign='center';}
  const cur=buildType!=null?'crosshair':(towerAt(hoverTile)?'pointer':'default');
  if(cv.style.cursor!==cur)cv.style.cursor=cur;
}

/* ---------- input ---------- */
function towerAt(c,r){for(const t of towers)if(t.c===c&&t.r===r)return t;return null;}
function validTile(c,r){return c>=0&&r>=0&&c<COLS&&r<ROWS&&MAP[r][c]==='.'&&!pathSet.has(c+','+r)&&!towerAt(c,r);}
function syncCards(){cards.forEach((c,i)=>c.classList.toggle('sel',i===buildType));}
function toggleBuild(i){if(state!=='prep'&&state!=='wave')return;
  buildType=(buildType===i?null:i);towerSel=null;syncCards();sfx.select();}
function tryPlace(c,r){
  const def=TOWERS[buildType];
  if(!validTile(c,r)){sfx.err();return;}
  if(pellets<def.cost){sfx.err();popup(c+0.5,r-0.3,'NEED '+def.cost+'●','#ff6b7a',0.9);return;}
  pellets-=def.cost;
  towers.push({type:buildType,c,r,x:c+0.5,y:r+0.5,cd:0,timer:0,aim:-Math.PI/2,flash:0});
  const d=dotAt.get(c+','+r);if(d&&d.alive){d.alive=false;dotsLeft--;}
  ringFx(c+0.5,r+0.5,cell*1.4,'#3aff8c');spark(c+0.5,r+0.5,'#ffe135',6,2.5);sfx.build();hudSync(true);
}
function sellTower(t){if(!t)return;
  const v=Math.floor(TOWERS[t.type].cost*0.7);pellets+=v;
  towers.splice(towers.indexOf(t),1);towerSel=null;
  popup(t.x,t.y-0.4,'+'+v+'●','#ffcf9e',1);spark(t.x,t.y,'#8b96c9',8,2.5);sfx.sell();hudSync(true);}

window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  initAudio();
  const key=e.key.length===1?e.key.toLowerCase():e.key;
  if(key===' '||key.indexOf('Arrow')===0)e.preventDefault();
  const playing=state==='prep'||state==='wave';
  if(playing&&!paused){
    if(key==='ArrowUp'||key==='w')pac.dir=[0,-1];
    else if(key==='ArrowDown'||key==='s')pac.dir=[0,1];
    else if(key==='ArrowLeft'||key==='a')pac.dir=[-1,0];
    else if(key==='ArrowRight'||key==='d')pac.dir=[1,0];
  }
  if(key>='1'&&key<='4'&&(playing||state==='menu'))toggleBuild(+key-1);
  else if(key==='Escape'){buildType=null;towerSel=null;syncCards();}
  else if(key==='x')sellTower(towerSel);
  else if(key===' '){if(state==='menu')initRun();else if(playing&&!paused)rushWave();}
  else if(key==='Enter'){if(state==='menu'||state==='over')initRun();}
  else if(key==='p')togglePause();
  else if(key==='m')toggleMute();
  else if(key==='r'&&state!=='menu')initRun();
});
window.addEventListener('pointerdown',()=>initAudio(),{passive:true});

cv.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{
  const r=cv.getBoundingClientRect();mx=e.clientX-r.left;my=e.clientY-r.top;
  hoverTile=[Math.floor((mx-ox)/cell),Math.floor((my-oy)/cell)];
});
cv.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>hoverTile=null);
cv.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  initAudio();
  if(state!=='prep'||paused)return;
  const r=cv.getBoundingClientRect(),x=e.clientX-r.left,y=e.clientY-r.top;
  const c=Math.floor((x-ox)/cell),rr=Math.floor((y-oy)/cell);
  const t=towerAt(c,rr);
  if(t){towerSel=(towerSel===t?null:t);buildType=null;syncCards();sfx.select();return;}
  if(buildType!=null){tryPlace(c,rr);return;}
  towerSel=null;
});
cv.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{e.preventDefault();
  if(towerSel)sellTower(towerSel);else{buildType=null;syncCards();}});

cards.forEach((c,i)=>{
  c.addEventListener('click',()=>{toggleBuild(i);c.blur();});
  c.addEventListener('keydown',ev=>{if(ev.key==='Enter'||ev.key===' '){ev.preventDefault();toggleBuild(i);}});
});
el('btnStart').addEventListener('click',function(){initRun();this.blur();});
el('btnAgain').addEventListener('click',function(){initRun();this.blur();});
btnSnd.addEventListener('click',function(){toggleMute();this.blur();});
btnPause.addEventListener('click',function(){togglePause();this.blur();});

function togglePause(force){
  const want=(force===undefined)?!paused:!!force;
  if(!(state==='prep'||state==='wave'))return;
  if(want===paused)return;
  paused=want;
  pausedEl.classList.toggle('hidden',!paused);
  btnPause.textContent=paused?'RESUME':'PAUSE';
  try{if(AC){if(paused)AC.suspend();else AC.resume();}}catch(e){}
}
function toggleMute(){muted=!muted;
  if(master)master.gain.value=muted?0:0.5;
  btnSnd.textContent=muted?'SND OFF':'SND ON';}

/* ---------- HUD ---------- */
let lastScore='',lastPellets='',lastWave='',lastHearts=-1,lastFright='';
function hudSync(force){
  const s=String(Math.max(0,score|0)).padStart(6,'0');
  if(force||s!==lastScore){hScore.textContent=s;lastScore=s;}
  const pl=String(pellets|0);
  if(force||pl!==lastPellets){hPellets.innerHTML='<span class="pip"></span>'+pl;lastPellets=pl;}
  const wv=(state==='menu'||state==='over')?'—':String(state==='prep'?wave+1:wave);
  if(force||wv!==lastWave){hWave.textContent=wv;lastWave=wv;}
  if(force||hearts!==lastHearts){
    hHearts.innerHTML=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>'<span'+(i<hearts?'':' class="off"')+'>♥</span>').join('');
    lastHearts=hearts;}
  const fr=frightT>0?frightT.toFixed(1)+'s':'';
  if(fr!==lastFright){hFright.classList.toggle('hidden',!fr);hFrightT.textContent=fr;lastFright=fr;}
  cards.forEach((c,i)=>c.classList.toggle('poor',pellets<TOWERS[i].cost));
}

/* ---------- boot & loop ---------- */
function mkDemo(t,route,d){const def=GTYPES[t];
  return{type:t,route,d,sp:def.sp*0.9,pos:routePoint(route,d),ang:-Math.PI/2,scale:1,ph:Math.random()*7,inAura:false,flash:0,alive:true};}
demoG=[mkDemo('B',ROUTE_L,0),mkDemo('P',ROUTE_R,5),mkDemo('C',ROUTE_L,10)];
el('mBest').textContent='HIGH SCORE '+String(best).padStart(6,'0');

let last=performance.now();
function frame(now){
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  let dt=(now-last)/1000;last=now;dt=Math.min(dt,0.05);
  if(paused)return;
  T+=dt;update(dt);draw();hudSync(false);
}
resize();
window.addEventListener('resize',resize);
initDots();
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{
  if(document.hidden&&(state==='prep'||state==='wave')&&!paused)togglePause(true);});
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
